### APPENDIX GPR — WHAT WOULD IT TAKE TO PROVE "THE WORLD IS CRYSTALLIZED MEANING"? — A proof-requirements map for the Genesis thesis: three layers, one test, and two triadic anchors that both lead to the same (currently blocked) frontier  `GPR`
*`APPENDIX_GPR_GENESIS_PROOF_REQUIREMENTS.md`*

**A meta-level (L0) proof-requirements map that does not try to prove the Genesis "world is crystallized meaning" thesis but instead specifies exactly what a proof would require, decomposing the thesis into three layers of differing epistemic status.**

GPR is a methodological boundary note (Layer-0 meta) for Petar Nikolov's U-Theory Genesis thesis: that the world arises from a proto-superposition (a primary form of meaning) that crystallizes into matter, where superposition is an uncommitted virtual triad, nonlocality is an 'unripe Position' that has not paid its spatial price, and measurement ripens and fixes it. The appendix's editor-frame explicitly states it does NOT attempt to prove the thesis; it maps precisely what proof would require, converting 'we want to prove it' into 'here is what must be found.' Its central organizing principle is one test (Section 0): a claim is scientifically provable only if its negation would look different in an experiment; if 'the substrate is meaning' and 'the substrate is quantum information/amplitude' produce identical experiments, the claim is an ontology choice, not a theorem. The thesis is then decomposed into three layers with three epistemic statuses. Layer 1 (world from a quantum substrate; superposition/nonlocality primary; matter as stabilized excitations) is established physics (QFT, Wheeler's 'it from bit') and needs only re-framing in F/P/A vocabulary, not proof. Layer 2 (the substrate IS meaning; superposition is uncommitted meaning; nonlocality is unripe Position) is interpretation, unprovable by construction (it-from-bit/QBism lineage), making no distinct prediction. It supplies three triadic 'unripe -> ripe, pays its currency' sub-readings, each with a real physical anchor: Form <-> Time (mass as the rate of the Compton clock, omega_C = mc^2/hbar); Position <-> Space (confinement energy of localization, Heisenberg cost ~ hbar^2/(2 m dx^2)); Action <-> Energy (Landauer's principle and negentropy as the energy price of resolving indeterminacy). The appendix's novelty is its own honesty: it flags that each anchor over-reaches (confinement energy localizes a single particle but does not anchor nonlocality/entanglement; Landauer is for erasure, not for an outcome becoming definite). Layer 3 (crystallization as a new physical process with a meaning-set threshold) is the only route to proof but is currently blocked: when operationalized, all three anchors converge on objective-collapse / spontaneous-localization models (Penrose-Diosi OR, CSL spontaneous heating). It stresses this convergence carries ZERO confirmatory weight - it reflects that objective collapse is the only measurement ontology that yields new physics, and the framework (per QMC Section 5.3) contains no fundamental dimensionful constant to derive such a threshold.

**Core claims**

- The Genesis thesis is not one claim but three (L-1/L-2/L-3) with three different epistemic statuses; one cannot 'prove the bundle,' only advance each layer.
- Provability test (Section 0): a claim is scientifically provable only if its negation would look different in an experiment; identical experiments make 'meaning' vs 'quantum information' a choice of ontology, not a theorem.
- Layer 1 (quantum substrate; superposition/nonlocality primary; matter = stabilized excitations) is established physics and needs only re-framing in F/P/A terms, not new evidence.
- Layer 2 (the substrate IS meaning; nonlocality IS unripe Position) is interpretation unprovable by construction — in the it-from-bit/QBism lineage — because it makes no distinct prediction.
- Each triadic sub-reading has a genuine physical anchor, but an anchor is not a prediction.
- Layer 3 (crystallization as a new physical process with a meaning-set threshold) is the only route to proof and is currently blocked.
- All three triadic anchors, when operationalized, converge on the same frontier — objective collapse (Penrose-Diosi OR / CSL spontaneous heating).
- This convergence carries ZERO confirmatory weight: it is the geometry of the problem (objective collapse is the only measurement ontology that makes a new-physics prediction), not evidence the triad is special.
- U-Theory's own formalism (per QMC Section 5.3) contains no fundamental dimensionful constant to derive a collapse threshold — it can only postulate one.
- GPR must be kept separate from QTC-2: GPR is interpretation-by-construction with no distinct prediction, whereas QTC-2 is a real, falsifiable prediction (filing them together would erase the corpus's anti-pseudoscience discipline).

**Key constructs:** Section 0 'one test': provable iff the negation would look different in an experiment; Three-layer decomposition: L-1 (established physics) / L-2 (interpretation, unprovable by construction) / L-3 (real but currently blocked); Form <-> Time anchor: Compton clock omega_C = mc^2/hbar; E = gamma·mc^2 as the Time-price (mass = magnitude of Form); Position <-> Space anchor: Heisenberg dx·dp >= hbar/2, confinement/zero-point energy ~ hbar^2/(2 m dx^2); nonlocality = unripe Position (B_S); Action <-> Energy anchor: Landauer's principle (>= k_B T ln 2 per bit; Berut et al. 2012); 2nd law / negentropy (Schrodinger; Prigogine) (B_E); Penrose-Diosi OR collapse time tau ~ hbar/E_G (mass = Form -> 'bigger Form crystallizes sooner'); CSL spontaneous localization with spontaneous heating (Position/Action channels); v26 Invariant: Form<->Time · Position<->Space · Action<->Energy; Proof checklist (Section 5): (1) operational meaning-gradient/ripening cost in measurable units; (2) a fundamental scale the framework contains; (3) a distinct prediction; (4) survival of current CSL/DP bounds; Experimental constraints cited: Donadi et al. 2021 (excludes parameter-free Diosi-Penrose); CSL matter-wave interferometry bounds (>25 kDa molecules); LISA Pathfinder rotational noise (2025); underground radiation searches; One-line boxed result: 'three claims — one established, one unprovable-by-construction, one blocked — and proof means supplying the single distinct prediction the third still lacks.'

**Structure.** Section 0 defines the single provability test. Section 1 decomposes the thesis into three layers (L-1/L-2/L-3) in a status table. Section 2 establishes Layer 1 as the 'physics half' already owned. Section 3 develops Layer 2 (meaning-identification) with three triadic sub-readings — 3.1 Form<->Time/Compton clock, 3.2 Position<->Space/confinement energy, 3.3 Action<->Energy/Landauer-negentropy — each with its own anchor, anchor caveat, and honest status, closing with a Layer-2 verdict. Section 4 argues Layer 3 is the only route to proof and shows all three anchors converge on objective collapse, with a strong disclaimer that the convergence is geometry not evidence. Section 5 is the proof checklist (four required deliverables). Section 6 is the verdict per layer plus 'the honest path' and the directive to keep GPR separate from QTC-2. Section 7 lists relations; Section 8 gives the boxed one-line.

**Epistemic status.** Self-labeled Status: L0 (meta) — the appendix is ABOUT what proof requires; each cited layer carries its own level (L1/L2/L3). Layer 1 = established physics; Layer 2 = interpretation unprovable by construction (no distinct prediction — explicitly a property of the claim, not a failure of effort); Layer 3 = open but blocked (the route to proof exists but the framework cannot currently derive the needed prediction and experiment already pressures it). Maturity: methodological/spec, not a result and not a device — it deliberately produces no prediction and carries a binding editor's frame that it does not try to prove the thesis. Strong overclaim guard: convergence on objective collapse carries ZERO confirmatory weight.

**Relations.** Prerequisites/parents: APPENDIX_GEN (the Genesis bootstrap this would make physical), APPENDIX_MMT (the meaning<->matter conversion, L3), APPENDIX_QMC §5.1–§5.3 (measurement/collapse boundary; the no-fundamental-constant result the proof checklist relies on), APPENDIX_DIM, THE_MIRROR_THEORY (the compiled projection), APPENDIX_DP §DP-S6 ('entanglement = shared Form', complementing 'nonlocality = unripe Position'). It explicitly contrasts itself with APPENDIX_QTC_BENCH §4 (QTC-2: classical compressibility predicts quantum protectability, Spearman ~0.98 in simulation, hardware pending, explicit falsifier) — a real falsifiable prediction — and demands the two NOT be filed under one label. References QTC noise measurement as the 'compiling agent' of decoherence.

**Caveats.** The document is itself a caveat-document. It states the thesis cannot be proven as a bundle. Layer 2 makes no distinct prediction and is unprovable by construction. It catches its own anchors over-reaching: confinement energy localizes a single particle and does NOT anchor nonlocality (conflating single-particle delocalization with entanglement — two entangled photons can be perfectly localized yet maximally Bell-nonlocal); Landauer's bound is for erasure (a logically irreversible operation), not for an outcome becoming definite, so invoking it for 'definiteness costs energy' borrows authority for a claim it does not make and is exactly the contested objective-collapse point, not its resolution. It notes 'noise' (stochastic) and 'chaos' (deterministic-but-unpredictable) coincide only at the ontological level of indeterminate Action, not as the same dynamical object. The convergence on objective collapse has zero confirmatory weight. The framework contains no fundamental dimensionful constant (phi is dimensionless; kappa is a free power; ℳ's seconds are variable) to derive a collapse threshold; the Layer-3 frontier is already partly excluded by current CSL/DP experimental bounds.

## V. Compression & Recording Sub-series (FPC / QTC)

### APPENDIX FPC — TRIADIC COMPRESSION: Writing Arbitrary Information in the Triadic Language: a Form–Position–Action Codec over the Ternary Substrate  `FPC`
*`APPENDIX_FPC_TRIADIC_COMPRESSION.md`*

**An appendix specifying the FPA codec — a lossless (optionally structural/lossy) data-compression scheme that recursively maps Petar Nikolov's Form/Position/Action triad onto arbitrary information and stores it as three frequency-sorted, deduplicated sets plus a link stream.**

FPC (also called the FPA codec) frames compression as transcription into the Triadic Language: any realized, distinguishable information is decomposed into exactly three irreducible kinds — Form (what it is / scale-normalised identity, costing Time), Position (where it is / coordinate plus operational context, costing Space), and Action (what it does / the transform or relation it carries, costing Energy). The codec performs a multidimensional, recursive mapping of this triad onto an information 'volume' V, then writes the result as a four-part compressed object Z(V) = (D_F, D_P, D_A, T): a Form set, a Position-step set, and an Action set — each sorted by descending frequency with identical entries grouped — plus a link/token stream T that rebinds the three sets into the original whole. Forms are grouped up to a scaling constant or a relation (a shape and its enlarged/rotated copy share one orbit representative), and the most-repeating primitive in each set receives the shortest code. The central thesis is that F/P/A are 'like 0 and 1, but more fundamental': the appendix grounds this in the Peircean Reduction Thesis (Burch 1991; Löwenheim 1915; Quine 1954) already in the v26 monolith — every n-adic relation with n≥4 reduces exactly to triads, but no triadic relation (e.g. betweenness B(x,y,z)) reduces losslessly to dyadic ones. Therefore base-3 over Σ₃={F,P,A} is the minimal-yet-complete ('maximal-yet-minimal') structural alphabet, and binary (dyadic) coders recover triadic structure only indirectly as flat statistics. A second, weaker reading is radix economy: base 3 is the nearest integer to the continuous optimum e≈2.718, with a trit carrying log₂3≈1.585 bits. What is novel is the unification of compression, the F·P·A triad, and the corpus stability score: the same non-compensatory aggregator U=∛(U_F·U_P·U_A) scores per-channel compression efficiency, so compressibility becomes a stability signature feeding SSS/TPL. A verified pure-Python reference implementation achieves a 2.80× lossless ratio (saving 64.3%) on a small self-similar glyph scene, with U=0.622 landing just above the canonical φ⁻¹=0.618 threshold and the score correctly flagging Action (U_A=0.433) as the most irreducible channel.

**Core claims**

- Compression is re-expression of information in its own most fundamental coordinates: every datum answers three unavoidable questions — what continues (Form), where/in what context it is distinguishable (Position), what it can do or undergo (Action).
- FPC-1 (Dyadic Insufficiency, L1): a dyadic/binary alphabet cannot losslessly encode genuinely triadic relational structure without scaffolding, while every n≥4 relation reduces exactly to triads — making base-3 over Σ₃ the minimal complete structural alphabet (inherited Peircean Reduction Thesis).
- FPC-3 (Form Orbit Equivalence): two Forms are identical iff one maps to the other under an allowed group G of scaling constants and relations (f1 ~ f2 ⇔ ∃g∈G: f1 = g·f2); D_F stores one orbit representative, Action carries the relational part of the group element and scale carries the constant part.
- FPC-4 (Round-Trip Theorem, L1): for any finite volume V encoded in lossless mode, DECODE(ENCODE(V)) = V, because ENCODE is an invertible relabelling (permutation + telescoping deltas + bijective rank↔value dictionaries + retained residuals), not a projection.
- FPC-2 (Radix Economy, L2): base 3 minimises the radix-economy cost E(b,N)=b(⌊log_b N⌋+1) among integer radices (continuous optimum e≈2.718), giving a per-symbol — but only modest — efficiency reading of 'more powerful'.
- FPC is complementary to classical coders: it supplies a triadic, scale-/relation-aware factorisation (catching repeats that differ by a constant or relation, which byte-level coders miss) and can hand its three rank-streams to any entropy coder as a back end.
- Compressibility is a stability signature: the non-compensatory mean U=∛(U_F·U_P·U_A) makes FPC a front-end measurement for SSS and a runtime record for GSI-RTD.

**Key constructs:** Σ₃ = {F, P, A} — the ternary structural alphabet; each datum carries a type-trit; Atom A_i = (f_i, p_i, a_i, s_i, r_i) with position p_i=(q_i,c_i) = coordinate plus operational context; Compressed object Z(V) = (D_F, D_P, D_A, T) — Form set, Position-step set, Action set, link/token stream; Triadic token t_i = (fid_i, sid_i, pid_i, aid_i, r_i) — ranks into the dictionaries (rank 0 = most frequent = shortest code); U = ∛(U_F·U_P·U_A); δ = (max−min)/(max+0.01); TPL stability index SI = U/(1+δ)² ; φ⁻¹ = 0.618 stability threshold; reference run U=0.6220, δ=0.4352, U_F/U_P/U_A = 0.775/0.717/0.433; FPC-1 boxed law: n≥4 ⇒ reducible to triads; n=3 ⇏ dyadic ∴ F–P–A is maximal-yet-minimal; Radix-economy cost E(b,N)=b(⌊log_b N⌋+1); trit = log₂3 ≈ 1.585 bits; Betweenness counterexample B(x,y,z) — triadic relation provably not expressible by dyadic predicates; Measured result: 30 atoms, 1020→364 bits, 2.80× ratio, 90-trit skeleton @142.6 bits vs 180 bits; Three-pass algorithm in canonical order Action→Position→Form, then frequency-rank and emit; O(n log n) encode, O(n) decode; v26 invariant: Form ↔ Time · Position ↔ Space · Action ↔ Energy

**Structure.** §0 Executive Summary (codec definition, Peircean basis, headline 2.80× result). §1 Core idea: compression as transcription into the Triadic Language; FPC is the compressed record of TPL. §2 The ternary substrate — Σ₃ alphabet, FPC-1 (triadic irreducibility) and FPC-2 (radix economy) with an RH guardrail. §3 Formal model: information volume V, the atom A_i, recursion via RTD to depth d. §4 The compressed object — three ordered sets D_F/D_P/D_A plus link stream T and the triadic token. §5 Equivalence — FPC-3 Form Orbit Equivalence (constant scaling vs relation), lossless vs structural/'naked snapshot' modes. §6 The three-pass ENCODE/DECODE algorithm (pseudocode). §7 FPC-4 lossless round-trip theorem with proof sketch. §8 Verified runnable pure-Python reference implementation and measured results. §9 Complexity and where triadic compression wins/loses vs classical coders (Huffman, LZ, DCT/wavelet). §10 Triadic balance score linking to SSS and TPL. §11 Recursion and the N-adic (N=3,4,5) generalisation. §12 Integration with the U-Theory corpus. §13 Falsifiability matrix. §14 What the appendix does NOT claim. §15 Relations to other appendices. §16 One-line law. §17 References.

**Epistemic status.** Mixed, explicitly tiered per the project's RH (Reality Hardening) levels. Header status: L1 (lossless reconstruction theorem) + L2 (engineering codec) + L3 (the 'more-fundamental-than-binary' framing). Graded confidences: L1 (>90%) for the round-trip theorem (FPC-4) and the Peircean irreducibility citation (FPC-1); L2 (70–90%) for the cross-domain/practical compression claim and radix economy (FPC-2); L3 for the universal-substrate interpretation. Maturity: spec + a verified, deterministic, runnable reference result (not yet benchmarked against production codecs). Testable: lossless round-trip (demonstrated 🟢), triadic irreducibility (established via Löwenheim–Quine/Burch 🟢), base-3 radix minimum (standard result 🟢). Interpretation/open: beating byte-level coders (🟡 plausible, not benchmarked), U tracking compressibility (❓ open). An explicit RH guardrail caps the radix-economy advantage as per-symbol and modest, denies any ternary-hardware superiority claim, and a §14 scope-discipline banner lists what the appendix does not claim. No RUO / not-a-device banner is present (this is a representation/codec, not a measurement instrument).

**Relations.** Prerequisites/parents: THEORY_OF_EVERYTHING_v26_CORE_MEANING (the F·P·A triad and U=∛(FPA), the triadic necessity/reduction theorem at §0.4.5.4a.1), APPENDIX_GSI-RTD (Recursive Triadic Decomposition — FPC is RTD executed at the data layer), APPENDIX_TPL (Triadic Parametric Language — FPC is its compressed machine record/serialization), APPENDIX_NDT (N-Adic Decomposition — FPC is the N=3 member, NDT supplies N=4 tetradic and N=5 pentadic generalisations), APPENDIX_SSS (System Stability Score — shares the non-compensatory U aggregator; FPC is a compressibility-as-stability front-end). Siblings: APPENDIX_TAA (Form/Position/Action/Σ agents map onto the three passes plus a Σ-agent that assembles T), APPENDIX_DIM (structural/lossy mode = dimensionless-meaning skeleton, residual = dimensional remainder), APPENDIX_RH (provides level tags, guardrails, scope discipline). FPC positions itself as the data-layer/binary instance of RTD beneath TPL.

**Caveats.** The appendix carries its own scope discipline (§14, per RH): it does NOT claim FPC beats production codecs (zstd, PNG, FLAC) on arbitrary data — only on structured/self-similar/lattice/low-Action-variety data, and that practical claim is only L2 pending benchmarks. It does NOT claim ternary hardware is superior (FPC-2 is about representation economy, not silicon, and the per-symbol gain is modest). It does NOT claim F/P/A extraction is automatic for every domain — choosing the Form library, scaling constants, and Action/relation group is domain-specific engineering, an open problem shared with TPL. It does NOT elevate the structural-substrate interpretation above L3. Only FPC-4 (lossless theorem) and FPC-1 (irreducibility citation) are L1. The reference implementation is a single concrete instantiation (a 2-D glyph scene) using conservative fixed-width ranks (a lower bound on the achievable ratio; a real implementation would entropy-code the ranks), and FPC's ratio degrades to ~1 or worse on high-entropy noise where residuals dominate and no orbit sharing applies.

### APPENDIX QTC — QUANTUM TRIADIC CODEC: Superposition-Native Recording & Reproduction of Information by Dynamic Quantum Triadic Tokens; Recursive Triad Mapping; and Stabilization Reduction through Decoherence-Free Encoding — the N=5 (Pentadic) FPA Token  `QTC`
*`IBM\APPENDIX_QTC_QUANTUM_TRIADIC_CODEC.md`*

**The quantum (N=5) lift of APPENDIX_FPC: it replaces the bit with a dynamic Quantum Triadic Token (a qutrit-indexed F/P/A register) and argues that encoding information in relational/shared-Form degrees of freedom places it in a decoherence-free subspace, so part of the error protection becomes intrinsic to the encoding.**

QTC is the quantum lift of the classical triadic codec APPENDIX_FPC. Where FPC records arbitrary information as three sorted sets of Form/Position/Action primitives plus a link stream, QTC replaces the dyadic storage unit (the bit) with a dynamic Quantum Triadic Token (QTT): a qutrit-indexed register whose three irreducible kinds {F,P,A} can be held in superposition, |tau> = alpha|F> + beta|P> + gamma|A>, and whose links are carried by entanglement rather than explicit pid/fid indices. The central re-framing is ontological: memory is a recursive lattice in which every Position is itself a live, gate-evolvable token, not a passive cell holding a value. The record/reproduce model follows: WRITE = preparing the joint state of the dynamic tokens (state synthesis); REPRODUCE = guided Born-rule measurement = decode; EVOLVE = applying Actions as unitary gates in place; LINK = entanglement; DEDUP = a shared entangled reference (since no-cloning forbids copying). The central testable thesis, QTC-1, is the honest version of 'superposition reduces stabilization': arbitrary superposition is MORE fragile, but information encoded in relational/shared-Form degrees of freedom that lie in a decoherence-free subspace (DFS) of the device's dominant collective noise needs less active error correction, because protection is intrinsic. This is demonstrated (statevector, density-matrix, Qiskit/Aer, and real IBM QPU): the shared-Form logical state (|01>+|10>)/sqrt2 keeps fidelity 1.0000 under collective dephasing at every noise angle with zero active correction, while the exposed state (|00>+|11>)/sqrt2 collapses as cos^2(theta). What is novel and carefully disclaimed: the validated mechanism is a textbook two-qubit (dyadic) collective-dephasing DFS (Lidar-Chuang-Whaley 1998), so 'triadic' names the information ontology, not the protection — deleting every F/P/A mention leaves the physics unchanged. The genuine contributions are the two-sided hardware scope boundary (independent idle noise gives R = -0.21 to ~0, near-zero benefit; injected collective noise gives R = +0.97, near-total) and the compressibility-protectability correlation. A later section (8.4) constructs a genuine qutrit DFS (total-M=0 sector of two spin-1 qutrits, 3-dimensional, F=1.0000) where '3' is load-bearing for the dimension of the protected object and minimal carrier count — but not for the mechanism, which remains the standard fixed-M DFS.

**Core claims**

- The storage unit is a dynamic triadic token, not a bit (L2): memory is a recursive lattice where every Position is itself a live, superposition-capable, gate-evolvable triadic register, decomposing triadically down to the qutrit; there is no underlying 0/1 layer.
- A new record/reproduce model (L2): WRITE = prepare the joint token state; REPRODUCE = guided measurement = decode; EVOLVE = apply Actions as gates in place; LINK = entanglement; DEDUP = shared entangled reference (no-cloning forbids copying).
- Recursive triad mapping in superposition builds dynamic constructions (L2/L3): tokens compose into trees of entangled sub-tokens realising NDT's N^d branching as a register hierarchy — but NOT demonstrated; §4-§5 are design only and constrained by no-cloning.
- QTC-1: recording in the RIGHT superposition reduces stabilization burden (L3, mechanism L1) — only relational/shared-Form encoding in a DFS; arbitrary superposition is more fragile.
- Verified core: shared-Form (|01>+|10>)/sqrt2 keeps fidelity 1.0000 under collective dephasing at all angles with zero active correction, while exposed (|00>+|11>)/sqrt2 collapses as cos^2(theta) (mean 0.6423 over the grid, zero at theta=pi/2).
- Hardware two-sidedness: on ibm_marrakesh (Heron r2) independent idle noise gives R = -0.21 (and ~0 in the clean multi-rep Phase-3c sweep) — no passive benefit; injected collective noise gives R = +0.97 — near-total protection. Passive protection appears precisely when and only when noise symmetry matches the code.
- QTC is a representation/error-protection scheme, NOT a compressor: a DFS encodes 1 logical qubit in >=2 physical qubits (overhead, not savings) and does not beat Holevo/Shannon bounds; 'lift' denotes structural reuse of FPC's token model.
- A genuine qutrit DFS (§8.4) protects a true 3-dimensional logical unit (F=1.0000 exactly on the whole block) where '3' is load-bearing for the dimension of the protected object and the minimal carrier count — but provides no new mechanism.

**Key constructs:** Quantum Triadic Token (QTT): qutrit-indexed register lifting FPC token t=(fid,sid,pid,aid,r) to quantum registers, type-selector becomes a qutrit; Type qutrit: |tau> = alpha|F> + beta|P> + gamma|A>, |alpha|^2+|beta|^2+|gamma|^2=1; uniform amplitudes => imbalance delta=0 (links to SSS/FPC §10 balance); Full token state: |t> = sum_{f,p,a} c_{fpa} |f>_F |p>_P |a>_A, generally non-separable; separable = independent F/P/A, entangled = physical link (Entanglement = shared Form); QTC-1 (Relational Encoding => Reduced Stabilization): DFS encoding in shared-Form/collective-symmetric subspace reduces active QEC overhead; Collective-dephasing DFS = span{|01>,|10>} (total spin-z = 0); invariant up to global phase; Verified fidelity table: F(DFS)=1.0000 for all theta; F(exposed)=cos^2(theta), mean 0.6423; collapse to 0 at theta=pi/2; R_QTC hardware metric: -0.211 (independent idle noise) to +0.971 (collective injected noise, theta=pi/2); R ~ 0 in natural Phase-3c sweep; Holevo bound: <= log2(3) ~= 1.585 retrievable bits per qutrit — no unbounded lossless compression; RTD_q(V): recursive triadic decomposition into dynamic tokens to depth d, realising NDT's N^d branching as a register hierarchy; Quantum<->triad dictionary: superposition=off-axis token; entanglement=shared Form; decoherence=Action leakage (Lindblad/Z_A dissipation); measurement=decode; DFS=Form-link collective noise is blind to; B_X (anti-entropy/QEC tax, N=4) and B_Y (coherence currency, N=5) bookkeeping from NDT/DIM — labeled as adding no predictive power; Genuine qutrit DFS (§8.4): total-M=0 sector of two spin-1 qutrits {|+1,-1>,|0,0>,|-1,+1>}, 3-dimensional logical qutrit, F=1.0000 (max|1-F|=1.1e-15); QTC-COMPILER: TPL -> FPC -> QTC -> readout toolchain (qtc_compiler.py); shared Form lifted to DFS bond = 1 ebit vs 9 classical index bits; transpile depth 5 on ['rz','sx','x','cx']

**Structure.** §0 Executive summary (FPC lift; not-a-compressor scope banner; four level-tagged claims; honest two-qubit-DFS scope). §1 From bits to dynamic triadic tokens (QTC-D1). §2 The QTT — type qutrit (2.1), full token state (2.2), and §2.3 the honest scope of the 'triadic' claim (the validated core is dyadic). §3 Record/reproduce model table (DECODE=MEASUREMENT). §4 Recursive triadic mapping in superposition (L3, not demonstrated; no-cloning constraint). §5 Dynamic constructions (Action=gate, composition, query-before-readout); §5.1 QTC-COMPILER verified toolchain. §6 QTC-1 stabilization reduction — honest claim (6.1), mechanism (6.2), NDT currency reading (6.3). §7 Quantum<->triad dictionary. §8 Verified DFS demonstration with code and fidelity table; §8.1 Phase-1 density-matrix suite (five channels A-E); §8.2 Phase-2 Qiskit/Aer; §8.3 Phase-3 hardware on ibm_marrakesh (negative idle result R=-0.21, Phase-3b collective R=+0.97, Phase-3c natural R~0); §8.4 a genuine qutrit DFS (how far '3' is load-bearing). §9 Resource limits & honesty (Holevo, no-cloning, threshold theorem, substrate requirement). §10 Corpus integration. §11 Falsifiability matrix. §12 What this appendix does NOT claim. §13 Relations to other appendices. §14 One-line law. §15 References.

**Epistemic status.** Mixed and explicitly per-component tagged. L1 (>90%): the DFS demonstration (§8) and Holevo/no-cloning/threshold limits (§9), being established quantum physics (Lidar-Chuang-Whaley 1998); also the §8.4 qutrit-DFS numerical simulation. L2 (70-90%): the QTT codec/architecture; B_X/B_Y currency bookkeeping (explicitly flagged as adding no predictive power); substrate requirement. L3: the claim that recursive triadic recording materially reduces real-device QEC overhead, and the recursive register-tree (§4-§5, marked NOT demonstrated). Testable vs interpretation is sharply separated: the DFS fidelity behavior and hardware R-values are measured/falsifiable; the F/P/A triadic framing is declared organizational ontology that does no physical protective work ('if every mention of F/P/A were deleted from §8, the physics would be unchanged'). Maturity: simulation- and hardware-demonstrated mechanism on a dyadic code, with the full engineering/triadic claim still open. Carries multiple honesty banners (editor's caveat at top, not-a-compressor scope, §2.3 honest scope, §12 scope discipline, §14 measured-boundary note) but no explicit RUO / not-a-device banner.

**Relations.** Direct parent: APPENDIX_FPC — QTC is its N=5 quantum lift (dynamic tokens replace bits, entanglement replaces explicit links; reuses FPC's F/P/A dictionary/token model). Dependencies/prerequisites: APPENDIX_NDT (N-adic family N=3->5, QEC as anti-entropy B_X tax that QTC-1 reduces, substrate requirement §3.2); APPENDIX_DIM (5D Coherence/Entanglement currency B_Y the token spends); APPENDIX_DP §DP-S6 / FH-QE (Entanglement=shared Form, decoherence=Action leakage, the quantum<->triad dictionary §7); APPENDIX_QC_NISQ_TRIADIC_ARCHITECTURE (the device-level recording substrate; QC-P7/MELQ as the route to collective noise); APPENDIX_TPL (surface triadic language QTC stores, verified through QTC-COMPILER). Siblings/uses: APPENDIX_SSS (token balance/delta scores representation stability §2.1; LGP-10 delta-monitoring loop reused as delta_q-triggered DD); APPENDIX_TAA (F/P/A agents prepare/measure registers, Sigma-agent assembles links); APPENDIX_RH (hardening/level tags, guardrails on QTC-1 in §6.1/§12). Roadmap: QTC-BENCH C-series for a qutrit DFS; Phase 3 hardware on IBM Quantum.

**Caveats.** The document is unusually self-critical. (1) The validated protection is a two-qubit (dyadic) collective-dephasing DFS — established physics, not a discovery; it does not depend on the number 3, so 'triadic' names the information ontology, not the mechanism. (2) Arbitrary superposition is MORE fragile, not less — QTC-1 holds ONLY for noise sharing the code's collective symmetry. (3) On today's superconducting transmons native idle noise is independent, so passive DFS encoding buys ~0 (R = -0.21 single-rep, ~0 multi-rep); benefit (+0.97) appears only under matching collective noise (engineered correlation, trapped ions, or a real 2-node MELQ shared bath). (4) Not a compressor: DFS encoding is protection overhead (1 logical qubit in >=2 physical), and Holevo caps retrievable info at log2(3)~1.585 bits/qutrit — no compression advantage. (5) The recursive register-tree and dynamic constructions (§4-§5) are design, not demonstrated, and constrained by no-cloning. (6) The B_X/B_Y currency labels are framework bookkeeping with no predictive power over the standard QEC statement. (7) Even the §8.4 qutrit DFS earns '3' only for the dimension of the protected object and minimal carrier count — not necessity (4 qubits also work), not a new mechanism, not a hardware result, and it protects collective S_z only (independent and collective S_x noise break it). Stabilization is reduced, not eliminated; QTC-1 lowers QEC cost but does not repeal the threshold theorem.

### APPENDIX QTC-BENCH — Benchmark Protocol for Triadic Quantum Recording: Turning QTC-1 from a DFS Demonstration into a Measurable Engineering Program — the FPC → QTC → NDT Bridge  `QTC-BENCH`
*`IBM\APPENDIX_QTC_BENCH.md`*

**A benchmark suite and falsifiability framework that converts the QTC quantum-triadic codec from a mechanism demonstration into a measurable engineering program, centered on the QTC-2 prediction that classical FPC compressibility forecasts quantum protectability.**

QTC-BENCH defines the measurement objects that move the QTC (Quantum Triadic Codec) appendix from architecture toward an empirical program. Where APPENDIX_QTC proved a mechanism (decoherence-free, shared-Form encoding survives collective noise with no active correction), QTC-BENCH makes that mechanism quantifiable: a runnable benchmark suite (qtc_bench.py, executed 31 May 2026, all asserts pass) that measures how much stabilization the triadic encoding saves, where it stops working, and whether a dataset's classical compressibility predicts its quantum protectability. The central thesis is the QTC-2 hypothesis: the more a dataset's FPC representation compresses by shared Forms and repeated relational Actions, the more efficiently its QTC lift can be placed into symmetry-protected / DFS-like degrees of freedom, so the active error-correction tax B_X falls (C_FPC up ⇒ B_X^QEC down for a matching relational noise model). The headline simulated result: across a structure sweep, FPC compressibility C_FPC and the stabilization-tax reduction R_QTC co-move with Spearman 0.983 (strictly monotonic, saturating once everything is protectable; Pearson is depressed to 0.705 by that saturation ceiling). The protocol comprises tests T1–T5 plus the QTC-2 sweep: T1 shows DFS lifetime invariance under collective dephasing (F=1.0 for all sigma) while exposed qubits decay to 0.50; T2 establishes the honest boundary that DFS is NOT protected against amplitude damping (mandating an active layer); T3/4 trace the robustness curve R_QTC(eta) from 1 to 0 as noise becomes non-collective; T5 shows an FPC index link can be encoded as one DFS-protected ebit (reconstruction fidelity 1.0). What is novel is the bridge claim itself — that classical factor-structure compressibility is a forecaster of physical quantum protectability — plus the pentadic QTC-SSS token-stability score (U_QTT = fifth-root of U_F·U_P·U_A·U_X·U_Y) extending the non-compensatory geometric mean to two substrate currencies (anti-entropy B_X and coherence B_Y). The document is deliberately hardened with a No-Go box (Holevo bound, no-cloning, QEC not removed, no claim that present hardware suffices) and an explicit falsifier: if the monotonic coupling vanishes on real hardware under a truly collective channel, QTC-2 is falsified.

**Core claims**

- QTC-2 (centerpiece): classical FPC compressibility predicts quantum protectability — C_FPC↑ ⇒ B_X^QEC↓ for a matching relational noise model.
- In simulation the coupling between C_FPC and R_QTC is strictly monotonic at Spearman 0.9832 (Pearson 0.7048), with the lower Pearson explained by saturation once protected_fraction reaches 1.0 (R_QTC plateaus at 0.925 while C_FPC keeps climbing).
- DFS / shared-Form encoding gives lifetime invariant fidelity (F=1.0 for all sigma) under collective dephasing because the collective channel is blind to the shared Form, while exposed tokens decay to 0.50.
- DFS protection is bounded: it is NOT protected against amplitude damping (F drops 1.00→0.50 as gamma 0→0.5), an honest limit that mandates an active QEC layer.
- R_QTC, the stabilization-tax reduction ratio, runs from 1 (fully collective → full passive protection) to 0 (fully independent → no advantage); QTC reduces, never zeroes, the active B_X tax.
- An FPC index link (2 classical index bits) can be encoded as 1 DFS-protected ebit, so a classical correlation becomes a physical, protected entanglement link (reconstruction fidelity 1.0).
- QTC is ternary in alphabet (qutrit type-selector over {|F>,|P>,|A>}) but pentadic in substrate solvency (currencies B_S,B_T,B_E,B_X,B_Y) — distinction QTC-D2.
- The pentadic token-stability score is non-compensatory: a single failing currency collapses U_QTT, so high entanglement cannot mask low gate fidelity.

**Key constructs:** QTC-2 hypothesis: C_FPC↑ ⇒ B_X^QEC↓ (compressibility forecasts protectability); Spearman(C_FPC, R_QTC) = 0.9832; Pearson = 0.7048 (synthetic sweep); gzip-vs-R_QTC Spearman 0.99 / Pearson 0.83 on real files; R_QTC = (B_X^exposed − B_X^DFS) / B_X^exposed, with B_X ≈ 1 − F (infidelity the active layer must repair); QTC-SSS pentadic aggregator: U_QTT = fifth-root(U_F·U_P·U_A·U_X·U_Y); delta_QTT = (max U_i − min U_i)/(max U_i + eps); SI_QTT = U_QTT/(1+delta_QTT)^2; QTC-D2: QTT ternary in alphabet (qutrit Sigma_3) and pentadic in substrate solvency (B_S,B_T,B_E,B_X,B_Y); Five currencies: U_F identity/Form, U_P Position localization, U_A gate/Action, U_X entropy-export/QEC efficiency, U_Y coherence/entanglement; Benchmark tests T1 (DFS vs exposed lifetime), T2 (amplitude-damping boundary), T3/4 (imperfect-collective robustness R_QTC(eta)), T5 (FPC link → ebit), QTC-2 sweep over structure parameter p; Canon invariant preserved: Form↔Time · Position↔Space · Action↔Energy, with Coherence/Entanglement as N=5 currency B_Y; QTC No-Go box: Holevo bound (≤ log2(3) bits/qutrit), no-cloning, QEC not removed, only relational/symmetry-protected encodings stabilized, NDT ascent is separate L3

**Structure.** Twelve numbered sections plus references. §0 Executive Summary (mechanism → measurable, headline Spearman 0.983); §1 QTC-D2 (ternary-alphabet vs pentadic-substrate distinction); §2 the benchmark protocol table (T1–T5 + QTC-2); §3 measured results from qtc_bench.py (T1 lifetime, T2 amplitude-damping boundary, T3/4 robustness table, T5 link→ebit); §4 QTC-2 the relational compression–stabilization coupling with the structure sweep table, Spearman/Pearson statistics, reading of the saturation effect, status and falsifier; §5 R_QTC the stabilization-tax reduction ratio definition; §6 QTC-SSS pentadic token stability score; §7 noise→triadic failure taxonomy table; §8 the QTC No-Go box (RH hardening); §9 dependency graph FPC→QTC→NDT; §10 falsifiability matrix; §11 relations & integration table; §12 one-line law; then References.

**Epistemic status.** Self-labeled multi-level: L1 (simulation results & physical bounds), L2 (benchmark protocol & token-stability score), L3 (QTC-2 coupling on real hardware, pending). Testable now: T1/T2/T3/4/T5 are marked Demonstrated (green) in the falsifiability matrix as simulation/density-matrix results with all asserts passing. The centerpiece QTC-2 is explicitly yellow — 'Simulation: Spearman 0.98; hardware pending' — supported only in simulation (L2), including real-file gzip tests that still use a noise simulation rather than hardware logical-error metrics. Maturity: a runnable, asserts-passing benchmark spec plus simulated results, not a hardware result. Carries an explicit RH-hardening No-Go box and a named falsifier (if monotonic coupling vanishes on real devices under truly collective noise, QTC-2 is falsified). Honest-limit framing throughout (e.g., T2 amplitude-damping boundary deliberately documents where DFS fails).

**Relations.** Child/benchmark layer of APPENDIX_QTC (the codec it measures; QTC §8.1/§8.2 are its first validations). Depends on APPENDIX_FPC as the source of C_FPC (classical base whose compressibility QTC-2 links to protectability). Builds on APPENDIX_NDT for the N=5 substrate and the B_X/B_Y currencies that R_QTC and QTC-SSS account, and on APPENDIX_DIM for the same substrate-currency vocabulary. Makes APPENDIX_DP §DP-S6 (the noise→triad dictionary) operational in §7. QTC-SSS is a child of APPENDIX_SSS (inherits the non-compensatory geometric-mean aggregator U=cbrt(U_F·U_P·U_A), extended to five currencies). APPENDIX_QC_NISQ supplies MELQ — the shared-Form DFS realised across nodes — as the Phase 3 hardware path. Companion runnable code: qtc_bench.py (this suite), qtc_validation_phase1.py, qtc_phase2_qiskit.py (Qiskit/Aer). Sits in the dependency chain CORE invariant → FPC → QTC → DFS/shared-Form → lower B_X tax → NDT N=5 substrate.

**Caveats.** The document is candid that the QTC-2 result is in-simulation only: even the real-file (gzip ratio) test uses a noise simulation, not hardware logical-error metrics, so the genuine test (Phase 3 / MELQ 2-node on real devices under a truly collective channel) is pending — and named as the falsifier. The No-Go box explicitly disclaims: storing infinite classical information (Holevo ≤ log2(3) bits/qutrit), cloning quantum Forms, removing active QEC (only reduces the B_X tax by R_QTC), stabilizing arbitrary superpositions (only relational/symmetry-protected encodings), proving cosmic dimensional ascent (NDT pathway is separate L3), replacing Shannon compression (FPC/QTC factor structure, not raw entropy), and claiming present-day hardware suffices (needs a coherence-solvent substrate). The Pearson coefficient of only 0.70 is acknowledged and attributed to saturation (protected_fraction capped at 1.0), with Spearman defended as the correct statistic for the monotonic 'more → more' claim rather than a linear one. DFS protection is scope-limited to noise the relational encoding is blind to (collective dephasing); it is bounded by amplitude damping and other non-collective channels.

### QTC — EXPERIMENT REPORT & SCALE-UP PLAN: What we ran on real quantum hardware, and how to make the benefit large  `QTC`
*`IBM\QTC_EXPERIMENT_DESIGN_AND_SCALEUP.md`*

**A hardware experiment report plus staged scale-up plan for the QTC (Quantum Triadic Code) thesis, showing on a real IBM QPU that triadic shared-Form / decoherence-free-subspace recording protects information exactly when the noise is collective.**

This appendix reports what was actually run on IBM quantum hardware to test the QTC thesis — that recording information in a triadic, relational, shared-Form superposition (rather than in fragile bit-like local amplitudes) lowers the active error-correction 'stabilization tax' — and then lays out a concrete, falsifiable plan to make the benefit large. The experiment compares two ways of storing one logical qubit in two physical qubits: an Exposed bit-like encoding {|00>,|11>} (total-Z = +/-2) versus a DFS / shared-Form encoding {|01>,|10>} (total-Z = 0, invariant under collective Z rotation). Using a Loschmidt-echo protocol (prepare |+_L>, idle or inject collective RZ(theta), uncompute, measure), it defines the figure of merit R_QTC = (B_X_exposed - B_X_DFS)/B_X_exposed with B_X ~= 1 - P(|00>). Three runs on ibm_marrakesh (Heron r2, 156 qubits) bracket the claim: native idle noise gives R = -0.21 (DFS slightly worse, noise not collective); injected collective RZ at theta=pi/2 gives R = +0.97 (DFS flat, exposed collapses as cos^2 theta); a no-injection delay sweep gives R_natural ~= 0 at all delays (the chip's idle noise is essentially fully independent). The central, honest finding: passive shared-Form/DFS protection is real but noise-symmetry-gated — it appears precisely when the noise has the matching collective symmetry. A follow-up C.3 collective-fraction sweep (mixing fraction eta of shared RZ with independent RZ) measured a clean crossover at eta* ~= 0.50: passive DFS pays off only when more than half the dephasing is collective; transmons sit near eta=0, ion traps near eta=1. The novelty is not the DFS physics (established prior art, Lidar-Zanardi-Whaley 1998; Knill-Laflamme-Viola 2000) but the U-Theory-specific reframing: the qutrit alphabet Sigma_3 = {|F>,|P>,|A>} carries semantic meaning (0,1,2 = Form, Position, Action), and the QTC-2 compression-to-protection bridge (FPC compressibility predicts QTC protectability), measured on real data as gzip-ratio vs R_QTC Spearman ~= 0.99.

**Core claims**

- Recording in a triadic shared-Form state (DFS {|01>,|10>}, total-Z=0) protects logical information against collective dephasing because the protected information lives in entanglement/relations, not fragile local amplitudes.
- On real IBM hardware (ibm_marrakesh, Heron r2), the stabilization-tax reduction ratio R_QTC ranges from ~0 under independent native noise to +0.97 under injected collective RZ at theta=pi/2.
- Passive DFS protection is noise-symmetry-gated: it appears only when, and exactly when, the noise symmetry matches the encoded relation (collective).
- A measured collective-fraction sweep shows a crossover at eta* ~= 0.50 — passive DFS pays off only when >50% of the dephasing is collective; transmons (eta~=0) gain nothing, ion traps (eta~=1) gain a lot.
- The chip's natural collective-noise fraction is ~0 (R_natural ~= 0 across 0/20/40/80 us delays), so the large benefit requires a different platform regime, not a different transmon experiment.
- QTC-2 hypothesis: classical FPC compressibility predicts quantum protectability — measured as gzip-ratio vs R_QTC with Spearman ~= 0.99, Pearson ~= 0.83 across 8 real datasets (model level, noise-sim pipeline).
- The bold/novel parts are the semantic ternary assignment (0,1,2 = Form/Position/Action) and the compression-protection law; the DFS mechanism and qutrit carrier are reused prior art.

**Key constructs:** R_QTC = (B_X_exposed - B_X_DFS) / B_X_exposed, with B_X ~= 1 - P(|00>) (stabilization-tax reduction ratio; R=0 no benefit, R=1 full passive protection, R<0 DFS worse); Exposed encoding {|00>,|11>}, total-Z = +/-2 vs DFS/shared-Form encoding {|01>,|10>}, total-Z = 0 (invariant under collective Z, picks up only global phase); Loschmidt/echo protocol: prepare |+_L> -> idle OR injected collective RZ(theta) -> uncompute -> measure; P(|00>) = echo fidelity; benefit ~ (collective-noise fraction) x (fraction of information held in protected relational DOF); Measured crossover eta* ~= 0.50 from C.3 sweep: R_QTC = -20.3 (eta=0) -> -0.01 (0.50) -> +0.96 (eta=1); Run 3b table: R_QTC(collective, pi/2) = +0.97; exposed follows cos^2(theta) collapse; Sigma_3 = {|F>,|P>,|A>} qutrit alphabet (0,1,2 = Form, Position, Action); QTT = semantic ternary token; QTC-2: C_FPC (classical compressibility) <-> R_QTC; gzip-ratio vs R_QTC Spearman ~= 0.99, Pearson ~= 0.83 (8 datasets); Dual-rail erasure code: {|01>,|10>} damping jump leaves subspace -> heralded erasure

**Structure.** Part A — the experiment in detail: the question, the ternary-core encoding under test, the Loschmidt-echo method and R_QTC metric, three runs (3a native idle R=-0.21; 3b injected collective R=+0.97; 3c natural-collective-fraction R~=0), what it proved, and honest limits. Part B — why a bigger benefit needs a different regime (collective fraction ~0 on transmons; three levers: platform, scale, metric). Part C — scale-up experiment plan, each with platform/setup/metric/expected effect/cost/falsifier: C.1 trapped-ion DFS T2 (headline, 10x-100x literature), C.2 multi-qubit collective-dephasing DFS scaling law, C.3 tunable collective-fraction sweep (MEASURED, eta*~=0.50), C.4 QTC-2 on real data (MEASURED, Spearman~=0.99 — the novelty test), C.5 dual-rail erasure + DD stack, C.6 logical metrics over many rounds. Part D — the 'ternary code with special meanings (0,1,2)' thesis, honestly split into what is genuinely novel, what is prior art, a new-vs-known DFS comparison table, and what would substantiate 'revolutionary'. Part E — staged roadmap with go/no-go decision gates (Stages 1-5) and the smallest high-value next step.

**Epistemic status.** Self-labelled mixed: L1 (measured hardware results: Runs 3a/3b/3c, C.3 sweep, C.4 real-data correlation), L2 (the scale-up experiment design), L3 (the 'semantic ternary substrate' thesis). Testable parts: the R_QTC ratios, the eta* crossover, and the gzip-vs-R_QTC correlation are measured with shot counts, error bars, scripts (qtc_hw_ibm.py, qtc_hw_collective.py, qtc_hw_phase3c.py, qtc_hw_c3_sweep.py, qtc2_realdata.py), and IBM/*.txt provenance; each scale-up experiment carries an explicit falsifier. Interpretation: the F/P/A semantic typing and the 'revolutionary' framing are aspirational until QTC-2 holds as a real predictive law, a semantic-ternary advantage over generic qutrits is shown, or large logical-lifetime gains appear. Maturity: one established-physics demonstration (DFS reproduced honestly) plus a clear falsifiable path to the novel parts. Honest one-liner included: QTC does not discover DFS; it reinterprets/integrates them and validates the boundary condition. No explicit RUO / not-a-device banner; CC BY 4.0.

**Relations.** Implements and reports on APPENDIX_QTC and APPENDIX_QTC_BENCH (the QTC thesis and benchmark). Depends on APPENDIX_FPC (FPC-1 Peircean irreducibility / radix-economy argument; FPC compressibility C_FPC feeding the QTC-2 bridge). Unifies a triadic framing FPC -> QTC -> NDT and ties into SSS accounting (the B_X anti-entropy / stabilization tax). Sits in the IBM hardware-experiment branch of the U-Theory v26/v27 corpus; extends established external prior art (Lidar-Chuang/Zanardi-Whaley 1998 collective-dephasing DFS, Knill-Laflamme-Viola 2000 noiseless subsystems, dynamical decoupling, 2023-24 dual-rail erasure detection) by adding the two-sided hardware scope boundary, the compressibility-protectability correlation, and the F/P/A semantic token ontology.

**Caveats.** The document is explicit about its limits: only two physical qubits / one logical qubit (the smallest possible code); echo-fidelity at a few delays, not a logical lifetime or multi-round QEC syndrome rate; the +0.97 collective benefit used INJECTED noise, not native correlated noise; the chip's natural collective fraction is ~0 so no benefit is seen on transmons in the wild. The DFS physics is established prior art — reproduced, not discovered. QTC-2 is supported only at the model level: gzip and the 4-byte relational redundancy both just measure redundancy by different algorithms, and R_QTC there is still a noise SIMULATION — the deep physical claim needs the hardware logical-error version (Stage 3+). The word 'revolutionary' is flagged as aspirational; the honest status is a bold, coherent, falsifiable reframing with one established-physics demonstration and a clear path to test the novel parts. Total QPU usage was tiny (~50 s of a 600 s budget across the three runs; 29 s for the C.3 sweep).

### TECHNICAL REPORT — Quantum Triadic Codec (QTC): A Superposition-Native Recording Architecture that Reduces the Quantum Stabilization Tax  `QTC`
*`IBM\QTC_TECHNICAL_REPORT.md`*

**A technical report presenting and hardware-validating the Quantum Triadic Codec, a recording architecture that stores information in relational/shared-Form (entangled) degrees of freedom so that the dominant collective device noise is blind to it, lowering the active error-correction tax.**

This technical report formalizes and validates the Quantum Triadic Codec (QTC), a recording architecture that stores information not in bits but in dynamic triadic tokens — qutrit-indexed registers whose three irreducible kinds (Form, Position, Action) can be held in superposition and whose links are carried by entanglement. The problem it addresses is the dominant cost of fault-tolerant quantum computing: stabilization, i.e. active quantum error correction (QEC) that continuously spends measurement and entropy budget to keep fragile amplitudes alive — cast in U-Theory terms as the 'anti-entropy tax' B_X paid to keep the coherence currency B_Y solvent. The central thesis is that if information is recorded in relations (shared Form = entanglement) rather than bits, it sits in a decoherence-free subspace (DFS) that collective noise cannot corrupt, so part of the error protection becomes intrinsic to the encoding and the active stabilization tax drops. The mechanism is the span{|01>,|10>} (total spin-z = 0) subspace, which under collective dephasing acquires only a global phase; in triad language this is 'one shared Form with two Position-references,' identified with the Mirror-Entangled Logical Qubit (MELQ) of APPENDIX_QC_NISQ. A five-experiment density-matrix validation suite confirms the mechanism: the shared-Form logical state holds fidelity 1.0000 under collective dephasing of arbitrary strength with zero active correction (Exp A); the same {|01>,|10>} code simultaneously heralds amplitude-damping jumps via a total-Z parity (Action) check, giving post-selected fidelity 1.0000 (Exp C); a delta-triggered dynamical-decoupling loop matches dense CPMG with 4x fewer pulses (Exp D); and a Holevo ledger bounds retrieval at log2(3) = 1.585 bits/qutrit (Exp E). What is novel is the unifying triadic framing that recognizes passive Form-protection and active Action-detection as the same code on two channels, plus an honest, hardware-validated demonstration on IBM hardware. The report is explicit that the advantage is in stabilization and structure, not retrievable bits — the Holevo bound is respected throughout, and the underlying techniques (DFS, noiseless subsystems, DD, dual-rail) are acknowledged prior art; the contribution is conceptual/organizational rather than a new physical speedup.

**Core claims**

- Recording information in relational / shared-Form (entangled) degrees of freedom places it in a decoherence-free subspace that the dominant collective device noise cannot corrupt, so part of error protection is intrinsic to the encoding and the active stabilization tax drops.
- The naive reading 'superposition reduces stabilization' is false in general (arbitrary superposition is MORE fragile); the defensible claim is specifically about symmetry-protected, relational superposition encoding.
- Passive Form-protection is exact and free against collective dephasing: shared-Form (MELQ) logical state holds fidelity 1.0000 for every dephasing strength sigma with zero active correction (Exp A); the exposed reference degrades to 0.5000.
- One code serves two channels: span{|01>,|10>} is simultaneously the collective-dephasing DFS and a dual-rail amplitude-damping detecting code — a jump leaves the total-Z=0 subspace and is heralded by the parity/Action check, giving post-selected fidelity 1.0000 with yield 1-gamma (Exp C).
- Adaptive stabilization is cheaper: a delta-triggered dynamical-decoupling loop (fires only when an imbalance metric rises) matches dense CPMG with 4x fewer pulses (2 vs 8) (Exp D).
- QTC lowers the QEC tax but does not repeal it: passive DFS covers only the noise it is symmetric to; residual non-collective noise still needs active codes.
- The advantage is stabilization-tax reduction plus structure, not extra retrievable bits — Holevo limits retrieval to log2(3) ~ 1.585 bits per qutrit.
- Hardware runs bracket the claim: R_QTC = -0.21 under independent T1/T2 noise (no benefit, as predicted) and R_QTC = +0.97 under injected collective Z noise — protection is real and noise-symmetry-gated.

**Key constructs:** Quantum Triadic Token (QTT): qutrit type-register alpha|F> + beta|P> + gamma|A> plus value-registers over Form/Position/Action dictionaries; Classical FPC token t=(fid,sid,pid,aid,r) lifted field-by-field to quantum registers; Decoherence-free subspace span{|01>,|10>} (total spin-z = 0), acquiring only a global phase under collective dephasing; Mirror-Entangled Logical Qubit (MELQ) = 'one shared Form with two Position-references' (from APPENDIX_QC_NISQ §QC.13.3); Entanglement = shared Form (binding of which Form, at which Position, with which Action is a quantum correlation, not an index); Total-Z parity (Action) leakage/jump-detection check for amplitude-damping heralding; delta-triggered dynamical-decoupling (DD) loop (SSS/LGP-10 delta-monitoring loop applied to coherence); Holevo bound: <= log2(3) ~ 1.585 retrievable bits per qutrit; Anti-entropy tax B_X paid to keep coherence currency B_Y solvent (from APPENDIX_NDT §4.3); Non-compensatory triad score U = cbrt(F * P * A) (from APPENDIX_SSS); N-adic recursion: token-tree branching by N to depth d (N^d leaves), N=3 -> 5 family; Hardware metric R_QTC: -0.21 (independent noise) to +0.97 (collective noise)

**Structure.** Eight sections plus header/abstract. Header gives author (Petar Nikolov, 31 May 2026), framework (U-Theory v26/v27), companion appendices, reference code files, and a status banner (L1/L2/L3). §1 Problem & Thesis frames stabilization as the anti-entropy tax and states the relational-superposition thesis. §2 Architecture (in brief) defines the QTT, dynamic-token memory lattice, links-as-entanglement, record=preparation / reproduce=measurement, and N-adic recursion. §3 explains the stabilization-reduction mechanism via the {|01>,|10>} DFS / MELQ. §4 presents the five-experiment density-matrix validation as a table (channels A-E with triadic defense and measured result) plus four key findings. §5 Honest Limits (per APPENDIX_RH) lists Holevo, no-cloning, threshold-theorem, substrate, and L3-status caveats. §6 Roadmap reports Phase 1 (density-matrix), Phase 2 (Qiskit/Aer), Phase 3 / 3b / 3c real-QPU runs on IBM ibm_marrakesh (Heron r2), a practical-benefit summary, and patches in flight. §7 places QTC as the N=5 pentadic lift of FPC within U-Theory. §8 gives the one-line summary and references (Burch, Lidar-Chuang-Whaley, Knill-Laflamme-Viola, Holevo, Wootters-Zurek, Nielsen-Chuang, plus U-Theory appendices).

**Epistemic status.** Self-declared mixed level in the status banner: L1 (validation results & physical bounds), L2 (codec architecture), L3 (the real-device stabilization-reduction claim until hardware validation). Testable/result-grade: the five density-matrix experiments (deterministic, all asserts pass), the Qiskit/Aer Phase 2 simulation, and the IBM hardware runs (Phase 3: R=-0.21 independent, Phase 3b: R=+0.97 collective, Phase 3c: R_natural ~ 0 on transmons). Interpretation/framing: the triadic Form/Position/Action mapping and the U-Theory currency framing. Maturity is mixed spec + result: mechanisms are validated in simulation and partially on hardware, while a true cross-chip MELQ 2-node remains future work. Honest-limits section invokes APPENDIX_RH. The report carries an explicit overclaim guard: its contribution is 'conceptual/organizational value, not a new physical speedup,' and the underlying techniques are established prior art.

**Relations.** QTC is presented as the N=5 (pentadic) lift of FPC: FPC (APPENDIX_FPC_TRIADIC_COMPRESSION, classical base) writes arbitrary information as three sorted sets plus links; QTC makes those tokens dynamic qutrit states and the links entanglement. Full spec lives in the companion APPENDIX_QTC_QUANTUM_TRIADIC_CODEC. It draws coherence-currency from APPENDIX_DIM (5D B_Y), generalizes via APPENDIX_NDT (N-adic family, anti-entropy tax), realizes APPENDIX_DP's 'entanglement = shared Form' (incl. §DP-S6), reuses APPENDIX_QC_NISQ's MELQ, applies the SSS/LGP-10 delta-monitoring loop to coherence, and scores compressibility/balance with the non-compensatory U=cbrt(F*P*A) of APPENDIX_SSS. Honest limits are governed per APPENDIX_RH. Reference implementations: qtc_codec_reference.py (DFS round-trip), qtc_validation_phase1.py (5-experiment suite), fpc_codec_reference.py, qtc_phase2_qiskit.py, qtc_hw_ibm.py, qtc_hw_collective.py, qtc_hw_phase3c.py.

**Caveats.** The document is candid about scope. Holevo: <= log2(3) ~ 1.585 bits/qutrit — no unbounded lossless classical compression; QTC buys stabilization-tax reduction and structure, not extra retrievable bits. No-cloning: a superposed Form cannot be copied; dedup becomes a shared entangled reference. Threshold theorem: passive DFS protects only noise it is symmetric to; residual noise still needs active QEC — QTC lowers the tax, it does not repeal QEC. DFS is not magic (Exp B: DFS degrades to F~0.85 at lambda=0.3 under independent dephasing/amp-damping). Substrate: a real QTT needs a coherence-solvent substrate (qutrits / qubit-pairs); classical simulation illustrates but does not deliver the benefit. The real-device claim was L3 until hardware; hardware then showed honestly negative/null results on transmons (R=-0.21 under independent noise; R_natural ~ 0 on ibm_marrakesh — no free benefit on transmons), with benefit only under injected collective noise (R=+0.97). A true cross-chip MELQ 2-node needs quantum networking beyond the current free plan / IBM cloud. Explicit prior-art acknowledgment (DFS: Lidar-Zanardi-Whaley 1998; noiseless subsystems: Knill-Laflamme-Viola 2000; DD ~2-10x T2 in practice; dual-rail erasure detection): the work's contribution is unifying triadic framing plus honest hardware-validated demonstration plus a falsifiable QTC-2 hypothesis — conceptual/organizational, not a new physical speedup.

### Noise-Symmetry-Gated Decoherence-Free Encoding on a Superconducting Quantum Processor — A controlled hardware study, with a compressibility–protectability correlation  `QTC`
*`IBM\TECHNICAL_NOTE_DFS_noise_symmetry.md`*

**An engineering technical note reporting a two-sided IBM-hardware demonstration that passive decoherence-free-subspace (DFS) protection helps only when device noise shares the code's symmetry, with a measured crossover at collective fraction eta* ~= 0.5 and a model-level classical-compressibility-to-quantum-protectability correlation.**

This note is an explicitly engineering result (not new physics) run on the IBM ibm_marrakesh superconducting processor (Heron r2, 156 qubits) using qiskit 2.4.1, qiskit-aer, and qiskit-ibm-runtime. It encodes one logical qubit in two physical qubits in two ways: a decoherence-free (DFS) code in the collective-dephasing-invariant subspace span{|01>,|10>} and an 'exposed' code span{|00>,|11>}. Using a Loschmidt-echo protocol (prepare logical |+>, idle or injected dephasing, uncompute, measure; ideal evolution returns |00>, so P(|00>) = logical fidelity), the authors measure each code's fidelity under (a) the device's native idle noise and (b) injected collective dephasing, and sweep the collective fraction eta of an injected mixture. The central figure of merit is the stabilization-tax-reduction ratio R = 1 - (1 - F_DFS)/(1 - F_exposed), where R=0 is no benefit, R=1 is full passive protection, and R<0 means DFS is worse. The key findings: under native (independent) noise the DFS gives no benefit (R ~= 0 to -0.21); under injected collective dephasing at theta=pi/2 it gives near-total protection (R = +0.97); and R(eta) crosses zero at eta* ~= 0.5. The DFS and exposed codes behave as mirror images, so passive DFS pays off only when more than half the dephasing is collective. Native transmon noise is measured to sit at eta ~= 0 (so DFS is not a free lunch on today's transmons), while trapped-ion noise sits near eta ~= 1, where large DFS gains are expected. Pure-numpy density-matrix (Phase 1) and Aer noise-model (Phase 2) simulations reproduce the hardware results, and the dual-rail code additionally heralds amplitude-damping jumps via a total-Z parity check (erasure detection), giving post-selected fidelity ~0.95-1.0 at yield 1-gamma. A secondary contribution (Section 4) reports a strong rank correlation between a dataset's classical compressibility and its protectability R under a fixed noise model: Spearman ~= 0.98 (synthetic structure sweep) and ~= 0.99 (real files plus controls), with Pearson 0.83. The note is novel as a controlled, two-sided, reproducible hardware confirmation of established DFS theory that honestly reports a negative (native-noise null) result, plus a compressibility-protectability correlation offered as a hypothesis.

**Core claims**

- Passive symmetry-protected (DFS) encoding is useful if and only if the dominant device noise shares the code's symmetry — demonstrated cleanly two-sided on real hardware.
- Under the device's native (essentially independent) idle noise, the DFS gives no benefit: R ~= 0 across a delay sweep (within +/-0.02), and R = -0.21 at 40 us in the single-rep run (DFS slightly worse due to gate overhead and T1 asymmetry).
- Under injected collective dephasing the DFS gives near-total protection: R(collective, pi/2) = +0.97; DFS stays flat (sees only a global phase) while the exposed code follows cos^2(theta).
- Sweeping the collective fraction eta, R runs from strongly negative (eta=0, R=-20.3) through zero at eta* ~= 0.50 to ~+0.96 (eta=1); DFS and exposed are mirror images, so DFS pays off only when collective fraction exceeds ~0.5.
- Native transmon noise sits at eta ~= 0 (measured, not assumed); trapped-ion noise sits near eta ~= 1, giving a concrete admission criterion: passive DFS helps a platform only if its collective-noise fraction exceeds ~0.5.
- A model-level correlation holds between classical compressibility and quantum protectability: Spearman(C,R) = 0.98 (synthetic), Spearman(gzip-ratio, R) = 0.99 and Pearson = 0.83 (real files); already-compressed/random data give R=0, text/code 0.36-0.48, tilings 0.93.
- Numpy density-matrix and Aer noise-model simulations reproduce all hardware results; the {|01>,|10>} dual-rail code also heralds amplitude-damping jumps via total-Z parity, yielding post-selected fidelity ~0.95-1.0 at yield 1-gamma.

**Key constructs:** R = 1 - (1 - F_DFS)/(1 - F_exposed), with F = P(|00>); R=0 no benefit, R=1 full protection, R<0 DFS worse; DFS code = (|01>+|10>)/sqrt(2) in collective-dephasing-invariant subspace span{|01>,|10>}; exposed code = (|00>+|11>)/sqrt(2) in span{|00>,|11>}; Loschmidt-echo protocol: prepare |+_L> -> idle/injected dephasing -> uncompute -> measure; P(|00>) = logical fidelity; Collective fraction eta and crossover threshold eta* ~= 0.50 (zero-benefit point); Injected mixture (C.3): collective angle theta*eta on both qubits + differential angle theta*(1-eta) as +/- on the two qubits, theta=pi/2; Exposed code decay follows cos^2(theta); DFS flat under collective dephasing (global phase only); Dual-rail erasure detection: total-Z parity check heralds amplitude-damping jumps, post-selected fidelity ~0.95-1.0 at yield 1-gamma; Spearman(C,R) = 0.98 (synthetic), Spearman(gzip-ratio, R) = 0.99, Pearson = 0.83 (real files); Hardware: ibm_marrakesh Heron r2 156q, 2048 shots, 5 reps/config, opt_level-1, SamplerV2, total QPU usage ~80 s

**Structure.** Scope banner (engineering result, not new physics; DFS is prior art) -> Abstract -> 1. Background (DFS / noiseless-subsystem theory, collective dephasing, the two codes and 2-for-1 overhead) -> 2. Methods (encodings, Loschmidt echo, figure of merit R, injected-noise mixture C.3, runtime and simulation cross-checks) -> 3. Results (3a native 40 us single rep R=-0.21; 3c native delay-sweep R~=0 table; 3b injected collective dephasing table, R=+0.97; C.3 collective-fraction sweep table, the central result with eta*~=0.5; simulation agreement and dual-rail heralding) -> 4. Compressibility-protectability correlation (two pipelines, synthetic and real-file Spearman/Pearson results) -> 5. Discussion (noise-symmetry gate, not-a-free-lunch on transmons, ion-trap expectation) -> 6. Limitations -> 7. Conclusion -> Data & code availability -> Credential hygiene -> References [1-4].

**Epistemic status.** Self-labeled as an engineering technical note, not new physics; corpus project level roughly B3 (controlled hardware result with reproducible logs and simulation cross-checks). The core noise-symmetry-gating claim (3a/3b/3c/C.3) is directly measured on hardware and testable, with reported error bars (e.g. P(|00>) DFS 0.972 +/- 0.003 at theta=pi/2) and an honestly reported negative/null native-noise result. The note frames it as a hardware confirmation of established DFS theory [1,2], not a novel mechanism. The compressibility-protectability correlation (Section 4) is explicitly flagged as model-level (R from a noise simulation, both measures tracking redundancy via different algorithms) and is offered as a motivating hypothesis, not an established predictive law. Carries an explicit scope/RUO-style banner that the broader U-Theory motivation is deliberately omitted and a credential-hygiene note. Maturity: completed result with raw IBM job logs, not a spec.

**Relations.** Belongs to the QTC (quantum) thread of the corpus and to the IBM hardware-experiment family; companion scripts qtc_hw_ibm.py, qtc_hw_collective.py, qtc_hw_phase3c.py, qtc_hw_c3_sweep.py, plus simulators qtc_validation_phase1.py (Phase 1 numpy density matrix), qtc_phase2_qiskit.py (Phase 2 Aer noise model), qtc_bench.py, and qtc2_realdata.py, with provenance logs in IBM/qtc_hw_*_marrakesh.txt and raw logs in IBM/*.txt. It implements and validates the DFS encoding ideas against established prior art (Lidar-Chuang-Whaley [1]; Knill-Laflamme-Viola noiseless subsystem [2]; Holevo [3]; Nielsen & Chuang [4]). A separate (deliberately omitted) document covers the broader U-Theory motivation; the compressibility-protectability correlation links the quantum-protectability result to the corpus's classical compressibility/redundancy and triadic bit-accounting themes.

**Caveats.** The document itself lists clear limits: it is the smallest possible code (1 logical in 2 physical), measuring echo fidelity at a few delays, not logical T2 over many QEC rounds; the collective benefit (3b/C.3) used injected coherent dephasing rather than native correlated noise; the compressibility-protectability correlation (Section 4) is model-level with R from a noise simulation rather than measured logical error rates, so it motivates but does not establish a predictive law (the genuine test is logical error rates on real datasets on hardware); and there is no multi-node/cross-chip test (requires quantum networking unavailable on the open plan). The note repeatedly stresses it is an engineering result, not new physics, and that passive DFS is not a free lunch on transmons (native eta ~= 0); the eta*~=0.5 crossover is the concrete admission criterion. Ion-trap 10x-100x DFS coherence gains are cited from literature, not measured here; the natural next experiment is trapped-ion DFS.
